### 472. assistant (2026-06-21T19:00:38.197Z)

// A6: insert the plain-language Request-Access form copy (site.join.*) as the first key inside the
// `site` object of every catalog. en content everywhere — the access form is not yet translated on
// any locale (a future increment); en-seeding keeps web key-parity green.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const root = import.meta.dirname;
const LOCALES = join(root, "locales");

const joinObj = {
  gpuLabel: "Do you have a powerful computer that could process videos on its own?",
  gpuHint: "A gaming PC or laptop with a graphics card. No worries if not — we can run it in the cloud for you.",
  gpuYes: "Yes — a gaming PC or laptop with a graphics card",
  gpuUnsure: "Not sure",
  gpuNo: "No",
  gpuCloud: "No — please run it in the cloud for me",
  storageLabel: "Where do your match videos live? How would you share them?",
  storageHint: "(pick any)",
  storageGdrive: "Google Drive",
  storageOnedrive: "OneDrive / Microsoft 365",
  storageDropbox: "Dropbox",
  storageBucket: "A cloud storage bucket — advanced (e.g. Amazon S3, Google Cloud)",
  storageLocal: "SD card or the device itself",
  storageUnsure: "Not sure",
};

const lines = JSON.stringify(joinObj, null, 2).split("\n");
const body = lines.map((l, i) => (i === 0 ? l : "    " + l)).join("\n"); // +4 -> depth 2
const insertion = `    "join": ${body},\n`;
const ANCHOR = `  "site": {\n`;

for (const loc of ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const file = join(LOCALES, loc, "common.json");
  let text = readFileSync(file, "utf8");
  if (text.includes('"join"')) { console.log(`${loc}: already has join, skipping`); continue; }
  if (!text.includes(ANCHOR)) throw new Error(`${loc}: site anchor not found`);
  text = text.replace(ANCHOR, ANCHOR + insertion);
  const parsed = JSON.parse(text); // validate
  if (!parsed.site.join.gpuLabel || !parsed.site.record) throw new Error(`${loc}: join insert broke site`);
  writeFileSync(file, text);
  console.log(`${loc}: site.join added (${Object.keys(parsed.site.join).length} keys)`);
}

### 497. assistant (2026-06-21T19:07:08.724Z)

// A7: insert site.capture.diagram (the camera-placement diagram's caption + legend) as the first
// key of site.capture in each catalog. en/es/da/id <- en; kn <- Kannada; hi <- Hindi (reusing the
// A3 capture terms for consistency). The capture page is already localized (A3), so the diagram
// labels are localized too.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const root = import.meta.dirname;
const LOCALES = join(root, "locales");

const diagram = {
  en: {
    title: "Side view: where the camera goes",
    camera: "Camera — behind the baseline, elevated",
    angle: "Tilt ~30° down",
    frame: "Whole court in frame — players never cropped",
    net: "Net",
  },
  kn: {
    title: "ಪಕ್ಕದ ನೋಟ: ಕ್ಯಾಮೆರಾ ಎಲ್ಲಿ ಇರಿಸಬೇಕು",
    camera: "ಕ್ಯಾಮೆರಾ — ಬೇಸ್‌ಲೈನ್ ಹಿಂದೆ, ಎತ್ತರದಲ್ಲಿ",
    angle: "ಸುಮಾರು 30° ಕೆಳಕ್ಕೆ ಬಾಗಿಸಿ",
    frame: "ಇಡೀ ಕೋರ್ಟ್ ಫ್ರೇಮ್‌ನಲ್ಲಿ — ಆಟಗಾರರು ಎಂದಿಗೂ ಕತ್ತರಿಸಲ್ಪಡಬಾರದು",
    net: "ನೆಟ್",
  },
  hi: {
    title: "साइड व्यू: कैमरा कहाँ लगाएँ",
    camera: "कैमरा — बेसलाइन के पीछे, ऊँचाई पर",
    angle: "लगभग 30° नीचे झुकाएँ",
    frame: "पूरा कोर्ट फ़्रेम में — खिलाड़ी कभी कटें नहीं",
    net: "नेट",
  },
};
const pick = { en: "en", es: "en", da: "en", id: "en", kn: "kn", hi: "hi" };

const ANCHOR = `    "capture": {\n`;
for (const loc of ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const file = join(LOCALES, loc, "common.json");
  let text = readFileSync(file, "utf8");
  if (text.includes('"diagram"')) { console.log(`${loc}: already has diagram, skipping`); continue; }
  if (!text.includes(ANCHOR)) throw new Error(`${loc}: capture anchor not found`);
  const obj = diagram[pick[loc]];
  const lines = JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2).split("\n");
  const body = lines.map((l, i) => (i === 0 ? l : "      " + l)).join("\n"); // +6 -> depth 3
  const insertion = `      "diagram": ${body},\n`;
  text = text.replace(ANCHOR, ANCHOR + insertion);
  const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
  if (!parsed.site.capture.diagram.title || !parsed.site.capture.step1) throw new Error(`${loc}: diagram insert broke capture`);
  writeFileSync(file, text);
  console.log(`${loc}: site.capture.diagram added (${pick[loc]})`);
}

# Session handoff — khelsutra (Friendly & Multilingual workstream)

_Last updated: 2026-06-22, after A3/A6/A7 merged._

## You are here
- The marketing `site/` is now **trilingual end-to-end** and non-tech-friendly:
  - **B3** ([#70](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/70)): shared `t()`/`data-i18n` shim + en/kn/hi switcher + `server.js` negotiation; catalogs hoisted to repo-root `/locales` (D-CATALOG-HOME).
  - **A3** ([#74](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/74)): recording-education (homepage gist + capture checklist) + `site.*` marketing copy translated to **kn + hi**; Noto bundled on the site; shim gained `data-i18n-html`.
  - **A6** ([#75](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/75)): plain-language Request-Access GPU/storage questions (de-jargoned + GPU reassurance), en, translation-ready.
  - **A7** ([#76](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/76)): accessible camera-placement SVG diagram on the capture page, localized en/kn/hi legend.
- The SPA (`web/`) was already trilingual+ (en/kn/hi + es/da/id). All merged on green CI.
- kn/hi translations are **AI-drafted + adversarially verified → native review still pending** before a wide public launch.

## Next steps / open threads (tracker §7 of docs/FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md)
- **B5/B6 SPA-console native-review pass** — an adversarial audit during A3 found fixable kn/hi *console* translation bugs (brand transliteration "KhelSutra"→खेलसूत्र, "engine"/"server" jargon leak, a `min_shot_count` code-token leak, over-Sanskritized Hindi). Spawned as a separate task (worktree `khelsutra-i18n-review`, branch `fix/kn-hi-spa-console-native-review`).
- **A2** — localized WhatsApp share for the reel (SPA + site). Not yet started.
- **Access form kn/hi** — A6 left the Request-Access form English on all locales (a small future increment; `site.join.*` is en-seeded).
- **Native-speaker review** of ALL AI-drafted kn/hi catalogs (site + SPA) before public launch; owner font-glyph eyeball on a real device.

## Ramp-up kit
- `docs/FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md` — §7 tracker (source of truth), §6.5 negotiation matrix, §8 decisions, changelog.
- `/locales/en/common.json` — shared catalog: SPA console keys + `site.*` (nav/hero/record/capture/join/ownModel).
- `site/public/i18n.js` (shim: `t`, `data-i18n`, `data-i18n-html`, negotiation), `site/server.js` (Accept-Language), `site/scripts/copy-locales.mjs` (the one build step), `site/src/{i18n.shim,locales-drift,assets,server}.test.js`.
- [[catalog-home-and-i18n-layout]], [[shared-checkout-concurrency]].

## Key decisions (don't relitigate)
- **D-CATALOG-HOME**: catalogs at repo-root `/locales`; SPA imports; site mirrors via `copy:locales` + drift guard.
- Negotiation order (both surfaces): persisted (`khelsutra.lang`) → `?lang=` → navigator/Accept-Language → en; missing key → en, never a raw key.
- Marketing kn/hi translated; **access form is en for now**. es/da/id are SPA-only (site is trilingual; their `site.*` is en-seeded for key-parity).
- Rich inline markup (`<b>`) localizes via `data-i18n-html` (innerHTML from project-authored catalog — XSS-safe).
- **Process**: isolated `git worktree` per PR (shared checkout, rapid master churn); AI-draft translations → adversarial verify workflow → native review is a pre-launch (not pre-merge) gate.

1	// The compiled-reel result (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md P5): once Build → POST /api/compile succeeds,
2	// preview the reel inline (<video> over GET /api/highlights/...) and offer a download. The engine
3	// returns a server-local filepath, NOT a URL (main.py:394), so the SPA builds the URL itself. On a
4	// failure (e.g. the min_shot_count 400) we show the engine's reason — loud, never swallowed.
5	
6	import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
7	import { StatusChip, Spinner } from "./StatusChip";
8	
9	export type BuildState =
10	  | { status: "idle" }
11	  | { status: "building" }
12	  | { status: "done"; url: string; filename: string }
13	  | { status: "error"; error: string };
14	
15	interface Props {
16	  build: BuildState;
17	  onDismiss: () => void;
18	}
19	
20	export function CompileResult({ build, onDismiss }: Props) {
21	  const { t } = useTranslation();
22	  if (build.status === "idle") return null;
23	
24	  // Build state (A8): a visible "stitching…" card with an icon+text chip + reassurance, so the user
25	  // isn't staring at a frozen footer wondering whether anything is happening.
26	  if (build.status === "building") {
27	    return (
28	      <section data-testid="compile-result" className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-black/10 bg-paper p-4" aria-live="polite">
29	        <StatusChip tone="info" icon={<Spinner />} label={t("result.building")} />
30	        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink/60">{t("result.buildingReassure")}</p>
31	      </section>
32	    );
33	  }
34	
35	  const isError = build.status === "error";
36	  return (
37	    <section
38	      data-testid="compile-result"
39	      aria-live="polite"
40	      className={"mt-6 rounded-xl border p-4 " + (isError ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50" : "border-green/30 bg-green/5")}
41	    >
42	      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
43	        {/* Status as icon+text (never icon-only): ✓ ready / ⚠ failed (A8). */}
44	        <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold text-ink">
45	          <span aria-hidden="true" className={isError ? "text-red-700" : "text-green"}>{isError ? "⚠" : "✓"}</span>
46	          {build.status === "done" ? t("result.ready") : t("result.failed")}
47	        </h2>
48	        <button
49	          type="button"
50	          onClick={onDismiss}
51	          aria-label={t("result.dismiss")}
52	          className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-ink/50 hover:text-ink"
53	        >
54	          ✕
55	        </button>
56	      </div>
57	
58	      {build.status === "done" ? (
59	        <div className="mt-3">
60	          {/* eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/media-has-caption — a stitched rally reel has no captions */}
61	          <video
62	            data-testid="reel-video"
63	            src={build.url}
64	            controls
65	            playsInline
66	            className="w-full max-w-xl rounded-lg bg-black"
67	          />
68	          <a
69	            href={build.url}
70	            download={build.filename}
71	            className="mt-3 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-full bg-green px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#04140D]"
72	          >
73	            {t("result.download", { filename: build.filename })}
74	          </a>
75	        </div>
76	      ) : (
77	        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-800" data-testid="compile-error">
78	          {build.error}
79	        </p>
80	      )}
81	    </section>
82	  );
83	}

### 591. assistant (2026-06-21T22:07:27.244Z)

// A2 catalog keys:
//  - result.share / result.shareText  -> SPA "share YOUR reel" (all 6 SPA locales, real translations)
//  - site.reveal.share / .shareText    -> site "share the product" post-reveal (en/kn/hi real; es/da/id en-seed)
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const LOCALES = join(import.meta.dirname, "locales");

const result = {
  en: { share: "Share on WhatsApp", shareText: "I made my badminton highlights reel with KhelSutra 🏸 Try it: khelsutra.guru" },
  kn: { share: "WhatsApp ನಲ್ಲಿ ಹಂಚಿಕೊಳ್ಳಿ", shareText: "ನಾನು KhelSutra ಬಳಸಿ ನನ್ನ ಬ್ಯಾಡ್ಮಿಂಟನ್ ಹೈಲೈಟ್ ರೀಲ್ ಮಾಡಿದೆ 🏸 ಪ್ರಯತ್ನಿಸಿ: khelsutra.guru" },
  hi: { share: "WhatsApp पर शेयर करें", shareText: "मैंने KhelSutra से अपनी बैडमिंटन हाइलाइट रील बनाई 🏸 आज़माएँ: khelsutra.guru" },
  es: { share: "Compartir por WhatsApp", shareText: "Hice mi reel de jugadas de bádminton con KhelSutra 🏸 Pruébalo: khelsutra.guru" },
  da: { share: "Del på WhatsApp", shareText: "Jeg lavede min badminton-highlightreel med KhelSutra 🏸 Prøv det: khelsutra.guru" },
  id: { share: "Bagikan ke WhatsApp", shareText: "Saya membuat reel sorotan bulu tangkis dengan KhelSutra 🏸 Coba: khelsutra.guru" },
};

const revealEn = { share: "Share on WhatsApp", shareText: "Check out KhelSutra — it turns a full badminton match into a clean highlights reel 🏸 khelsutra.guru" };
const reveal = {
  en: revealEn,
  kn: { share: "WhatsApp ನಲ್ಲಿ ಹಂಚಿಕೊಳ್ಳಿ", shareText: "KhelSutra ನೋಡಿ — ಇದು ಪೂರ್ಣ ಬ್ಯಾಡ್ಮಿಂಟನ್ ಪಂದ್ಯವನ್ನು ಶುದ್ಧ ಹೈಲೈಟ್ ರೀಲ್ ಆಗಿ ಮಾಡುತ್ತದೆ 🏸 khelsutra.guru" },
  hi: { share: "WhatsApp पर शेयर करें", shareText: "KhelSutra देखें — यह पूरे बैडमिंटन मैच को एक साफ़ हाइलाइट रील में बदल देता है 🏸 khelsutra.guru" },
  es: revealEn, da: revealEn, id: revealEn,
};

const RESULT_ANCHOR = `  "result": {\n`;
const SITE_ANCHOR = `  "site": {\n`;

for (const loc of ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const file = join(LOCALES, loc, "common.json");
  let text = readFileSync(file, "utf8");

  if (!text.includes('"share"')) {
    const r = result[loc];
    const ins = `    "share": ${JSON.stringify(r.share)},\n    "shareText": ${JSON.stringify(r.shareText)},\n`;
    text = text.replace(RESULT_ANCHOR, RESULT_ANCHOR + ins);
  }
  if (!text.includes('"reveal"')) {
    const rv = reveal[loc];
    const ins = `    "reveal": {\n      "share": ${JSON.stringify(rv.share)},\n      "shareText": ${JSON.stringify(rv.shareText)}\n    },\n`;
    text = text.replace(SITE_ANCHOR, SITE_ANCHOR + ins);
  }
  const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
  if (!parsed.result.share || !parsed.site.reveal.share) throw new Error(`${loc}: A2 insert incomplete`);
  writeFileSync(file, text);
  console.log(`${loc}: result.share + site.reveal added`);
}

=== existing Playwright screencast script ===
// KhelSutra Studio — FULL bucket→reel CUJ, driven entirely from the UI (B-P2).
// Point at a gs:// bucket video → engine processes (real Gemini) → review rallies → build → download.
// Records the whole journey. Run against the SPA dev server (proxies /api → the live engine on :8000).
import { chromium } from "playwright";

const URL = process.env.URL || "http://localhost:5180";
const GS_URI = "gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/videos/mahadevpura_smoke_180s.mp4";
const PROCESS_TIMEOUT = 300000; // up to 5 min for the real Gemini run + gs:// fetch
const HOLD = 1800;

const browser = await chromium.launch();
// Fresh context, English browser → the SPA negotiates `en` (no persisted pref).
const ctx = await browser.newContext({
  locale: "en-US",
  viewport: { width: 1280, height: 900 },
  recordVideo: { dir: "video-cuj", size: { width: 1280, height: 900 } },
});
const page = await ctx.newPage();
page.on("console", (m) => { if (m.type() === "error") console.log("  [page error]", m.text().slice(0, 160)); });

await page.goto(URL, { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });

// 1) Land on the ingest screen (no ?video= → the "Process a video" panel).
await page.getByText("Process a video", { exact: true }).waitFor({ timeout: 15000 });
console.log("ingest panel rendered; lang =", await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.lang));
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
await page.screenshot({ path: "cuj-1-ingest.png" });

// 2) Paste the bucket URI (typed for the camera) and submit.
const input = page.getByLabel(/video location/i);
await input.click();
await input.pressSequentially(GS_URI, { delay: 28 });
await page.waitForTimeout(700);
await page.screenshot({ path: "cuj-2-uri.png" });
await page.getByRole("button", { name: /process video/i }).click();

// 3) Live progress while the engine fetches the bucket video + runs Gemini.
await page.getByText(/Processing your video/i).waitFor({ timeout: 15000 }).catch(() => {});
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
await page.screenshot({ path: "cuj-3-processing.png" });

// 4) Rallies load into the existing back-half (the ingest panel hands off video_id → ?video=).
await page.getByText(/rallies from this session/i).waitFor({ timeout: PROCESS_TIMEOUT });
const loaded = await page.getByText(/rallies from this session/i).textContent();
console.log("rallies loaded:", loaded?.trim());
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
await page.screenshot({ path: "cuj-4-rallies.png" });

// 5) Build the reel from the (default all-selected) rallies.
const buildBtn = page.getByRole("button", { name: /build reel/i });
await buildBtn.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
await buildBtn.click();

// 6) Reel ready → preview + download.
await page.getByText("Your reel is ready", { exact: true }).waitFor({ timeout: 120000 });
await page.getByTestId("compile-result").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
await page.screenshot({ path: "cuj-5-reel-ready.png" });

// 7) Download to the local machine (the real end of the CUJ).
const [download] = await Promise.all([
  page.waitForEvent("download"),
  page.getByRole("link", { name: /download/i }).click(),
]);
await download.saveAs("cuj-downloaded-reel.mp4");
console.log("downloaded reel:", download.suggestedFilename());
await page.waitForTimeout(1200);

await ctx.close(); // flushes the video
await browser.close();
console.log("DONE");

// Capture above-the-fold screenshots of the marketing site (headers + hero) across pages,
// viewports and locales — for the UI/UX header/clutter eval. Run with URL + OUT env.
import { chromium } from "playwright";
import { mkdirSync } from "node:fs";

const URL = process.env.URL || "http://localhost:8123";
const OUT = process.env.OUT || "eval-before";
mkdirSync(OUT, { recursive: true });

const pages = [["home", "/"], ["capture", "/capture"], ["own-model", "/own-model"]];
const views = [["desktop", 1280, 900], ["mobile", 390, 844]];
const locales = ["en", "kn"];

const browser = await chromium.launch();
for (const [vn, w, h] of views) {
  for (const loc of locales) {
    const ctx = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: w, height: h }, locale: loc === "kn" ? "kn-IN" : "en-US" });
    const page = await ctx.newPage();
    for (const [pn, path] of pages) {
      await page.goto(URL + path + "?lang=" + loc, { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" }).catch(() => {});
      await page.waitForTimeout(1000); // let fonts + the shim settle
      await page.screenshot({ path: `${OUT}/${pn}-${vn}-${loc}.png` }); // above-the-fold (header + hero)
    }
    await ctx.close();
  }
}
await browser.close();
console.log("shots ->", OUT);

### 672. assistant (2026-06-21T22:25:34.163Z)

Good steer — let me fold that in. The critique workflow is still running; here's my recommendation on the switcher (you invited pushback) and how I'll do the deep layout/render test.

**On moving the switcher out of the top panel — partial pushback, with a better middle option:**

What the screenshots already proved is the real bug: the home header **overflows on mobile** ("Request access" is clipped) and is crowded on desktop — because the switcher is **3 always-visible pills** (`English ಕನ್ನಡ हिन्दी`) competing with 6 nav links + the CTA. Capture and own-model headers are actually fine (own-model wraps; capture has room).

I'd **keep the switcher in the header but collapse it to a compact dropdown** — a single control showing 🌐 + the current language that reveals the three options on tap — rather than moving it to the footer. Reasoning:
- For a non-tech kn/hi coach, a **top, visible "ಕನ್ನಡ" cue is a welcome signal** ("this site speaks your language"); burying it in the footer means they'd scroll a wall of English to find it.
- It's the **conventional, discoverable** spot — and a single button fits mobile + desktop with room to spare, killing the overflow and the clutter.
- I'll build it no-JS-friendly (native `<details>` disclosure, keyboard/ARIA-correct), consistent across all three pages.
- (Auto Accept-Language negotiation already gives a Kannada phone Kannada automatically, so the switcher is mostly a manual override + signal — which the compact dropdown preserves.)

If you'd still rather it leave the header entirely, say so and I'll move it (e.g., a slim full-width language bar directly under the header) — but I think the compact dropdown is the cleaner win.

**Deep layout + rendering test (desktop + mobile, en + kn):** I'll drive Playwright across all 3 pages × {1280, 390} × {en, kn} and assert **zero horizontal overflow** (`scrollWidth ≤ clientWidth`), check tap-target sizes, and eyeball every screenshot for Indic-glyph rendering (no tofu) and alignment — before and after the fix — and save the captures locally.

I'll implement the compact dropdown + fold the critique findings, then run that full test and report before/after. Proceeding on that basis unless you redirect.

Full critique captured — strong consensus: **(1) critical** home-header mobile overflow (CTA clipped, worse in kn), **(2) major** 3 inconsistent headers + the switcher jumping position/style, desktop crowding (fragile in kn), capture h1 losing prominence on mobile, and switcher **tap targets < 44px**, plus the active pill competing chromatically with the CTA.

The root cause across nearly all of these is the **3-always-visible pills**. So I'll do exactly what I recommended — replace them with a **compact globe + `<select>`** (the robust, accessible, no-JS-correct form of a dropdown): it shows the current language (signal preserved), can't overflow, declutters desktop, is one comfortable tap target, and renders identically on every page. Plus defensive header fixes (wrap + `overflow-x` safety). Let me start with the shim:

### 677. user (2026-06-21T22:28:33.858Z)

71	// Walk i18n-tagged nodes under `root`. Three forms (missing key -> skip, leaving the HTML default):
72	//   data-i18n="key"                      -> textContent
73	//   data-i18n="key" data-i18n-attr="x"   -> setAttribute(x, …)  (placeholder / aria-label / alt …)
74	//   data-i18n-html="key"                 -> innerHTML  (for catalog-authored inline markup like <b>;
75	//                                           values are our own content, never user input)
76	export function applyDom(root, catalogs, locale) {
77	  for (const el of root.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n], [data-i18n-html]")) {
78	    const htmlKey = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
79	    const val = translate(catalogs, locale, htmlKey || el.getAttribute("data-i18n"));
80	    if (val == null) continue;
81	    const attr = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr");
82	    if (htmlKey) el.innerHTML = val;
83	    else if (attr) el.setAttribute(attr, val);
84	    else el.textContent = val;
85	  }
86	  for (const el of root.querySelectorAll("[data-lang]")) {
87	    const on = el.getAttribute("data-lang") === locale;
88	    el.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(on));
89	    el.classList.toggle("is-active", on);
90	  }

### 716. assistant (2026-06-21T22:34:42.271Z)

// Deep layout + rendering test: every page × {desktop,mobile} × {en,kn}. Asserts NO horizontal
// overflow (scrollWidth <= innerWidth), the compact <select> switcher reflects the locale, and
// captures an "after" screenshot for each combo.
import { chromium } from "playwright";
import { mkdirSync } from "node:fs";

const URL = process.env.URL || "http://localhost:8123";
const OUT = process.env.OUT || "eval-after";
mkdirSync(OUT, { recursive: true });

const pages = [["home", "/"], ["capture", "/capture"], ["own-model", "/own-model"]];
const views = [["desktop", 1280, 900], ["mobile", 390, 844]];
const locales = ["en", "kn"];

const browser = await chromium.launch();
const results = [];
for (const [vn, w, h] of views) {
  for (const loc of locales) {
    const ctx = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: w, height: h }, locale: loc === "kn" ? "kn-IN" : "en-US" });
    const page = await ctx.newPage();
    for (const [pn, path] of pages) {
      await page.goto(URL + path + "?lang=" + loc, { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" }).catch(() => {});
      await page.waitForTimeout(900);
      const m = await page.evaluate(() => ({
        sw: document.documentElement.scrollWidth,
        iw: window.innerWidth,
        lang: document.documentElement.lang,
        selVal: (document.querySelector("select[data-langsw]") || {}).value,
      }));
      const overflow = m.sw > m.iw + 1;
      results.push({ page: pn, view: vn, loc, ...m, overflow });
      await page.screenshot({ path: `${OUT}/${pn}-${vn}-${loc}.png` });
    }
    await ctx.close();
  }
}
await browser.close();

for (const r of results) console.log(`${r.overflow ? "OVERFLOW!" : "ok      "} ${r.page}/${r.view}/${r.loc}  lang=${r.lang} sel=${r.selVal} sw=${r.sw}/iw=${r.iw}`);
const bad = results.filter((r) => r.overflow);
console.log(bad.length ? `\nFAIL: ${bad.length} overflow case(s)` : "\nPASS: no horizontal overflow on any page/viewport/locale");

### 725. assistant (2026-06-21T22:37:42.070Z)

// Localize the home hero headline (the one big English string left on the kn/hi home page).
// Inserted as the first key of site.hero. en/kn/hi real; es/da/id en-seed (site is trilingual).
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
const LOCALES = join(import.meta.dirname, "locales");

const en = 'Every rally, indexed. <span class="hl">The dead time, gone.</span>';
const title = {
  en, es: en, da: en, id: en,
  kn: 'ಪ್ರತಿ ರ್ಯಾಲಿಯೂ ಸೂಚಿಯಲ್ಲಿ. <span class="hl">ನಿಷ್ಕ್ರಿಯ ಸಮಯ, ಮಾಯ.</span>',
  hi: 'हर रैली, सूची में. <span class="hl">बेकार समय, ग़ायब.</span>',
};
const ANCHOR = `    "hero": {\n`;
for (const loc of ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const file = join(LOCALES, loc, "common.json");
  let text = readFileSync(file, "utf8");
  if (text.includes('"hero": {\n      "title"')) { console.log(`${loc}: already has hero.title`); continue; }
  text = text.replace(ANCHOR, ANCHOR + `      "title": ${JSON.stringify(title[loc])},\n`);
  const p = JSON.parse(text);
  if (!p.site.hero.title || !p.site.hero.lead) throw new Error(`${loc}: hero.title insert broke hero`);
  writeFileSync(file, text);
  console.log(`${loc}: site.hero.title added`);
}

### 759. assistant (2026-06-22T01:00:56.806Z)

// Records two screencasts of the localized + decluttered marketing site:
//   1) desktop multilingual journey: home (en) -> compact switcher -> Kannada home -> capture page
//   2) mobile header: the clean compact switcher reflowing en -> kn -> hi (no overflow)
// Run against the site server (URL env). Outputs .webm into ./ (Playwright records webm natively).
import { chromium } from "playwright";
import { rename } from "node:fs/promises";
import { mkdirSync } from "node:fs";

const URL = process.env.URL || "http://localhost:8123";
const HOLD = 1600;
mkdirSync("rec", { recursive: true });
const browser = await chromium.launch();

async function record(name, viewport, steps) {
  const ctx = await browser.newContext({ viewport, locale: "en-US", recordVideo: { dir: "rec", size: viewport } });
  const page = await ctx.newPage();
  await steps(page);
  const v = page.video();
  await ctx.close(); // flush the video
  await rename(await v.path(), name);
  console.log("wrote", name);
}

// 1) Desktop: en home -> switch to Kannada -> recording gist -> capture page (kn persists)
await record("khelsutra-site-i18n-desktop.webm", { width: 1280, height: 800 }, async (page) => {
  await page.goto(URL + "/", { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });
  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
  await page.selectOption("select[data-langsw]", "kn");
  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
  await page.locator("#record").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
  await page.goto(URL + "/capture", { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });
  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
  await page.locator(".diagram").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
});

// 2) Mobile: the compact header switching en -> kn -> hi, never overflowing
await record("khelsutra-site-header-mobile.webm", { width: 390, height: 844 }, async (page) => {
  await page.goto(URL + "/", { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });
  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);

## Key decisions (don't relitigate)
- D-CATALOG-HOME: catalogs at repo-root `/locales`; SPA imports; site mirrors via `copy:locales` + drift guard.

- Switcher = compact `<select>` in the header (kept there for discoverability/signal; auto-negotiation covers kn-phones). Rich `<b>` markup localizes via `data-i18n-html` (XSS-safe; catalog-authored).
